```
def _apply_single(c_val, h_cur, p, delta):
    hp = h_cur[p]
    if abs(delta) < 1e-18: return
    # l=0 index n-1
    c_val[n-1] += (1 - 2*hp)*delta - delta*delta
    # left as p: lags l = from -p to n-1-p, exclude 0
    # q = p+l valid. contribution delta*(1-h[q])
    if p > 0:
       # l negative: q=0..p-1, l=q-p = -p..-1; index m = n-1+l = n-1-p..n-2
       c_val[n-1-p : n-1] += delta * (1 - h_cur[0:p])  # length p
    if n-1-p > 0:
       # l positive: q=p+1..n-1, l=1..n-1-p; index n..n+p-1? m=n-1+l => n..n+p-1
       c_val[n : n+p] += delta * (1 - h_cur[p+1:n])
    # right as y: x=p-l valid. For l negative: x=p-l>p? Let's derive.
```

```
def pair_move_max(c, h, i, j, da, dj):
  ct=c.copy()
  hc=h.copy() # or only need p value? Updates require h at other coords; if only two changed, can update hc [i,j]
  hc[p]=... But for slices, need updated values for i when updating j. If copy h length n. Could instead pass h and after first set `hc = h.copy()`; okay.
  apply_single(ct, hc, i, da); hc[i]+=da
  apply_single(ct, hc, j, dj); hc[j]+=dj
  return ct.max()
```

```
g = np.zeros(n)
for m in range(2n-1):
  if w[m] ==0 continue
  l = m-(n-1)
  wm = w[m]
  if l>=0:
     # i=0..n-l-1
     g[:n-l] += wm * (1-h[l:])
     if l>0: g[l:] += -wm*h[:n-l] # l=0: g[:]+= -wm*h
  else:
     kk=-l; # i=kk..n-1
     g[kk:] += wm * (1-h[:n-kk])
     g[:n-kk] += -wm*h[kk:]
```

Implementation of smooth max weights and gradient:
```
def smooth_weights(c, alpha):
  m=c.max()
  s=(c-m)/alpha
  s-=s.max()
  w=np.exp(s)
  ssum=w.sum()
  if ssum<=0: w[np.argmax(c)]=1
  else: w/=ssum
  return w, m
```
alpha in c units. Use alpha=20? Maybe c differences can be ~20. Let's choose adapt: `alpha = max(1e-8, (p99 - p95)/2?)` Hmm. For active lags, if multiple near max, alpha large includes. If c spread large, only max. That's okay; many active? Need if several equal maxima, alpha 10 includes within 10 units (undamped). C units difference would be alpha*dx=0.067, too broad; we want narrower. Alpha_c = 2? Difference in C 0.013. Good. Since c units: C difference 0.00005 => c diff=0.0075. So alpha_c=2 is broad. Use 1 or 5. If c max ~57, active lags may differ <1. Use alpha=2. Let's use `alpha_c = max(0.5, 5.0/n? no)`. Use 2.0. For L-BFGS need larger alpha for smooth objective; can use C units.

Gradient function with weights. For n=300, loop over 2n-1 and use if w>1e-12. Use `compute_grad_from_weights(h, w)`. For weights length 2n-1.

```
def grad_from_w(h,w):
 n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n)
 for m,wm in enumerate(w):
   if wm < 1e-14: continue
   l = m-(n-1)
   if l >=0:
     nv=n-l
     if nv>0:
       g[:nv] += wm*(1-h[l:])
       if l==0: g[:] += wm*(-h) # wait for l=0, first g[:]+=wm*(1-h); second should g[:]+= -wm*h. If combine, g[:]+=wm*(1-h); g[:]-=wm*h. Above if l==0 g[:]+= -wm*h.
       else: g[l:] += -wm*h[:nv]
   else:
     kk=-l; nv=n-kk
     g[kk:] += wm*(1-h[:nv])
     g[:nv] += -wm*h[kk:]
```
For l=0, first line g[:n] += wm*(1-h[n:n]?) h[l:]=h? OK. Second if l==0 g[:] += -wm*h, so total 1-2h.

For l<0, kk=-l, left contribution i=kk..n-1, q=i+kk? Actually l=-kk, q=i-l=i+kk valid <=n-1 -> i<=n-1-kk; first nv=n-kk; i indices kk..n-1? Wait if l=-kk, x=i, q=i+kk. def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,max_time=None):
```
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c = direct_corr(h)
    cmax=c.max()*? Use unscaled cmax = c.max()
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
    # Use c unscaled for line search; final C=best_c*dx
    # Parameters
    rng global? pass seed.
    while time.time()<deadline:
       # if no improvement after sweep, break
       cmax = c.max()
       # compute gradient with alpha
       alpha = choose 2,5,10
       w,_ = smooth_weights(c, alpha)
       g = grad_from_w(h,w)
       # gradient pairs
       low = np.argsort(g)[:K]
       high = np.argsort(-g)[:K]
       pairs = []
       for i in low: for j in high: if i!=j and g[i] < g[j]: pairs.append((i,j))
       # Also random pairs
       random_count = min(200, int(sqrt(n)*10))
       for _ in range(random_count): pairs.append(rng.integers(...))
       no_improve=True
       evaluated=0
       for (i,j) in pairs:
          if time>deadline: break
          # restrict search side based on gradient
          if g[i] < g[j]: lo=0; hi=min(h[j],1-h[i])
          elif g[i] > g[j]: lo=max(-h[i], h[j]-1); hi=0
          else: full
          # skip if interval tiny
          tlo,best_cm = pair_line_search(c,h,i,j,lo,hi,eval_limit=25)
          if best_cm < cmax - 1e-12:
              # apply if feasible
              h[i]+=tlo; h[j]-=tlo; project tiny? just clip and fix sum? t from feasible, no sum change.
              c = direct_corr(h); cmax=c.max()
              no_improve=False
              # update best if lower
              if cmax < best_c: best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
       if no_improve: break
    return best_h, best_c*dx
```

```
def eval_pair_move(c, h, i, j, t, n, cmax_old):
    if t == 0: return cmax_old
    ct = c.copy()
    ht = h.copy()
    # apply i delta t
    hp = ht[i]
    if t != 0:
       ct[n-1] += (1-2*hp)*t - t*t
       if i > 0:
          ct[n-1-i:n-1] += t*(1-ht[:i])
       ln = n-i-1
       if ln > 0:
          ct[n:2*n-i-1] += t*(1-ht[i+1:n])  # length ln
       if i > 0:
          ct[n:n+i] += -t*ht[:i]
       rn = n-i-1
       if rn > 0:
          ct[i:n-1][::-1] += -t*ht[i+1:n]
       ht[i] += t
    # apply j delta -t: delta = -t
    hpj = ht[j]
    dj = -t
    ... same with dj
    return ct.max()
```

```
def apply_single(ct, ht, p, delta, n):
    if delta == 0: return
    hp=ht[p]
    ct[n-1] += (1.0 - 2.0*hp)*delta - delta*delta
    if p > 0:
        ct[n-1-p:n-1] += delta * (1.0 - ht[:p])
    m = n - p - 1
    if m > 0:
        ct[n:2*n-p-1] += delta * (1.0 - ht[p+1:n])
    # right shifted
    if p > 0:
        ct[n:n+p] += -delta * ht[:p]
    if m > 0:
        # ct[p:n-1] reversed length m
        ct[p:n-1][::-1] += -delta * ht[p+1:n]
    ht[p] += delta
```

For p=n-1 => stop=2n-(n-1)-1 = n, start n length0.
Left negative slice: if p>0, start n-1-p, stop n-1 length p. For p=n-1, start0 stop n-1 length n-1, q=0..n-2; good.

```
def pair_min_t(c,h,i,j,lo,hi,max_eval=30):
    n=len(h)
    if hi - lo < 1e-14: return 0.0, c.max()
    # check endpoints and 0 maybe
    best_t=0.0; best_v=c.max()
    # evaluate lo,hi,0 if within interval and different
    for t in (lo,hi,0.0):
       if t < lo - 1e-15 or t > hi + 1e-15: continue
       t clipped
       v=eval...
       if v<best_v: best_v=v; best_t=t
    if max_eval <= 3: return
    # golden interval maybe restrict to [a,b]
    a=lo; b=hi
    gr=(np.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    c1=b-gr*(b-a); c2=a+gr*(b-a)
    v1=eval(c1); v2=eval(c2)
    evals=...
    while b-a > max(1e-13, 1e-12*max(1,abs(zERO?))) and evals<max_eval:
      if v1 <= v2:
         b=c2; c2=c1; v2=v1; c1=b-gr*(b-a); v1=eval(c1)
      else:
         a=c1; c1=c2; v1=v2; c2=a+gr*(b-a); v2=eval(c2)
      evals++
    # final mid
    t3=0.5*(a+b); v3=eval
    choose best among v1,v2,v3,endpoints
    return best_t,best_v
```

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
   s=n*0.5
   h=np.asarray(h,float)
   # If already bounds and sum close, fine
   lo=-10; hi=10? tau. We want x=clip(h - tau,0,1). The range of tau where sum changes [-max?]. Use lo=min(h)-1, hi=max(h)+1 maybe.
   lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
   hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0
   tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
   for _ in range(80):
      x=np.clip(h-tau,0,1); sm=x.sum()
      if sm < s:
         # need smaller tau to increase sum
         hi=tau
      else:
         lo=tau
      tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
   x=np.clip(h-tau,0,1)
   # adjust residual
   diff=s-x.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-15:
      # distribute to non-saturated maybe
      if diff>0:
         room=1-x; tot=room.sum(); if tot>0: x += diff/tot*room
      else:
         tot=x.sum(); if tot>0: x += diff/tot*x
      x=np.clip(x,0,1)
   return x
```
But if h has values far >1, min/max tau range okay. Bisection condition: sum(clip(h-tau)) decreasing in tau. If sm < s, sum too small, need decrease tau, so hi=tau? Suppose tau high gives low sum. import numpy as np
```
import time

DX constants.

def project_box_sum(h,n): ...
def direct_corr(h): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
def direct_c5(h): n=len(h); return float(np.max(direct_corr(h)) * (2.0/n))
def cmax_unscaled(h): return float(np.max(direct_corr(h)))
```

Smooth weights:
```
def smooth_weights(c, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <= 0:
       idx=np.argmax(c); w=zeros; w[idx]=1; return w,m
    s = (c - m) / alpha
    smax = s.max()
    w = np.exp(s - smax)
    ss = w.sum()
    if ss <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
       idx = int(np.argmax(c)); w.fill(0); w[idx]=1.0
    else: w /= ss
    return w, m
```
Gradient:
```
def gradient_from_weights(h,w):
   n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(len(w)):
      wm=float(w[m])
      if wm < 1e-15: continue
      l = m - (n-1)
      if l >= 0:
         nv = n - l
         if nv > 0:
            g[:nv] += wm * (1.0 - h[l:])
            if l == 0:
               g[:] += -wm * h
            else:
               g[l:] += -wm * h[:nv]
      else:
         kk = -l; nv = n - kk
         if nv > 0:
            g[kk:] += wm * (1.0 - h[:nv])
            g[:nv] += -wm * h[kk:]
   return g
```
Check l=0: `g[:nv]` where nv=n, g[:n] += wm*(1-h[l:]) = 1-h; then if l==0 g[:] += -wm*h. Good.

But for l>0, `g[:nv] += wm * (1.0 - h[l:])`: h[l:] length n-l = nv. good. `g[l:] += -wm*h[:nv]`: h[:nv] length nv. good.
For l<0, `g[kk:] += wm * (1.0 - h[:nv])`: h[:nv] length n-kk; g[kk:] length n-kk. good. `g[:nv] += -wm*h[kk:]`: h[kk:] length n-kk. good.

Apply single:
```
def apply_single(ct, ht, p, delta, n):
    if delta == 0.0:
       return
    hp = ht[p]
    ct[n-1] += (1.0 - 2.0*hp)*delta - delta*delta
    if p > 0:
       ct[n-1-p:n-1] += delta * (1.0 - ht[:p])
    m = n - p - 1
    if m > 0:
       # indices n to 2n-p-2 inclusive => stop 2n-p-1
       ct[n:2*n-p-1] += delta * (1.0 - ht[p+1:n])
    if p > 0:
       ct[n:n+p] += -delta * ht[:p]
    if m > 0:
       ct[p:n-1][::-1] += -delta * ht[p+1:n]
    ht[p] += delta
```
Need check `ct[n:2*n-p-1]` for p=n-1 m=0 no; for p=n-2 m=1 stop=2n-(n-2)-1=n+1, start n length1. good.
Potential issue: `ct[p:n-1][::-1] += -delta * ht[p+1:n]` when p=n-1 m=0 no. For p=n-2 m=1: ct[n-2:n-1] length1 reversed ok. For p=0 m=n-1: ct[0:n-1][::-1] add ht[1:n]. good.

Eval pair:
```
def eval_pair_move(c, h, i, j, t):
    n = len(h)
    if t == 0.0:
       return float(np.max(c))
    ct = c.copy()
    ht = h.copy()
    apply_single(ct, ht, i, t, n)
    apply_single(ct, ht, j, -t, n)
    return float(np.max(ct))
```
If i and j same? skip. If t not finite skip.

Line search:
```
def pair_line_search(c, h, i, j, max_eval=24):
    n = len(h)
    hi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
    lo = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
    # tolerance
    if hi - lo < 1e-14:
       return 0.0, float(np.max(c))
    base_v = float(np.max(c))
    best_t = 0.0
    best_v = base_v
    # check 0 and endpoints once
    for t in (0.0, lo, hi):
        if t < lo - 1e-12 or t > hi + 1e-12:
            continue
        if t < lo: t = lo
        elif t > hi: t = hi
        v = eval_pair_move(c,h,i,j,t)
        if v < best_v:
            best_v = v; best_t = t
    if max_eval <= 3:
       return best_t, best_v
    a = lo; b = hi
    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    # If base at 0 is best and derivative? we still search full. Golden from full.
    x1 = b - gr*(b-a)
    x2 = a + gr*(b-a)
    f1 = eval_pair_move(...)
    f2 = eval...
    evals=3

          b = x2
          x2 = x1
          f2 = f1
          x1 = b - gr*(b-a)
          f1 = eval...
       else:
          a = x1
          x1 = x2
          f1 = f2
          x2 = a + gr*(b-a)
          f2 = eval...
       evals += 1
    xm = 0.5*(a+b); fm=eval...
    # choose best among cached? best_t already. check x1,x2,xm.
    return best_t,best_v
```

```
def lbfgs_refine(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_box_sum(h,n)
    best=h.copy(); best_c=cmax_unscaled(h)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    # configs alpha,mu maybe tuples
    configs = [(5,1e-2),(10,1e-1),(20,1.0),(50,10.0),(200,100.0),(1000,1000.0)]
    # but alpha too high makes objective near max, gradient active; mu large enforce sum. Could be okay.
    for alpha,mu in configs:
       if time.time() > deadline-5: break
       maxiter = 80 if n<=400 else 40
       try:
          res = minimize(_lbfgs_obj, h, jac=_lbfgs_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':maxiter*3})
          h2 = project_box_sum(res.x,n)
          c2 = cmax_unscaled(h2)
          if c2 < best_c:
             best=h2; best_c=c2
             h=h2
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best, best_c
```

`h_j - t ≤1 => -t ≤1-h_j => t≥h_j-1`; `h_j-t≥0 => t≤h_j`.

Implement `fix_sum_exactish`:
```
def adjust_sum(h,n):
    s = 0.5*n
    diff = s - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) < 1e-13: return h
    # if positive add to non-saturated
    h = h.copy()
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # use projection if not tiny
    if abs(diff) > 1e-9:
        return project_box_sum(h,n)
    # distribute to a few indices
    for i in range(n):
       if h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-15 and diff >0:
          add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[i]); h[i]+=add; diff-=add; if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
       elif h[i] > 1e-15 and diff <0:
          sub = min(-diff, h[i]); h[i]-=sub; diff+=sub; if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    return h
```
Use after pair accept to fix tiny. But if no saturated and diff tiny, add to first could slightly change objective; but only 1e-15. Fine.

Maybe avoid adjusting every accept; sum drift only rounding. Recompute c after with drift maybe sum not exactly. Objective doesn't enforce sum in our c; but task final requires. Final adjust.

During pair search, if sum drifts, direction mass conservation may fail? We only use t, so sum unchanged mathematically. Floating addition `h[i]+=t; h[j]-=t` preserves sum to within rounding. Good.

```
def _lbfgs_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100
   c=direct_corr(x)
   w,m = smooth_weights(c,alpha) # m unused
   # compute smooth val using weights? val = np.dot(w,c) if weights normalized? Since smooth max logsumexp not equal dot if shifted? Actually softplus-like: softmin? If weights normalized, logsumexp = m + alpha*log(sum exp(...)/? Need not exact. For objective, we can just use `np.dot(w, c)`? Is that a smooth max? The softmax max approximation is m + (1/alpha) log sum exp((c-m)/alpha). If weights = exp((c-m)/alpha)/sum, then logsumexp = m + alpha*log(sum weights)?? Since sum weights=1, log(1)=0, not correct. Need not match gradient? We can define objective as weighted average ∑w c with w normalized. Its gradient is ∑(dw/dc_j)c_j + w_j, not equal ∑w grad c_j. So don't use dot. Use logsumexp formula and gradient from weights (which is gradient of logsumexp scaled? derivative of m + (1/alpha)logsumexp((c-m)/alpha) wrt c is weights? Let's verify: f(c)=max? This is smooth (except m? logsumexp formula using m exactly equals logsumexp(c) which is smooth? logsumexp(c) is smooth; representation using m is okay because m is argmax but logsumexp(c) smooth? At points where two equal, m changes but logsumexp(c) continuous differentiable; computing with m numerically okay). Gradient = normalized exp(alpha(c-m)). Good.
```

Let's derive deltas for a pair move (i + t, j - t). For each lag l, A_l(t) = old c_l + Δ_l(t). The delta as function t can be computed analytically using current h values. This is more efficient and exact. Let's do it!

For pair (i,j), moved variables with signs s_i=+1, s_j=-1 (t). For each lag m (l), old c_l = ∑_{x∈S} h_x - h_x h_{x+l}. Delta from variable p with change δ_p = s_p t if p in overlap.
As before for a single variable p change δ:
- l=0: Δ = (1-2h_p)δ - δ².
- l≠0: if p as left (q=p+l valid): δ*(1-h_q); if p as right (x=p-l valid): -δ*h_x.
For two variables, sum of single deltas with old h? If using old h for both independently, need cross terms when both appear in same product. The linear deltas using old h plus for l where i and j paired: product cross term? Let's derive Δ_l(t) directly.
For lag l≠0.
Terms: linear L_l = ∑_{x∈S} h_x. Delta linear = t*(s_i if i∈S else 0 + s_j if j∈S else 0) = L1 t.
Product P_l = ∑_{x∈S} h_x h_{x+l}. Delta product we need subtract: ΔA = ΔL - ΔP.
Product terms affected:
- term (i, i+l) if valid and i+l != i: old h_i h_{i+l}; new (h_i+s_i t) h_q if q not j, or (h_i+s_i t)(h_j+s_j t) if q=j. Delta = s_i t * h_q + (if q=j) cross s_i s_j t². If q=j, h_q old = h_j; linear coefficient s_i t*h_j, cross s_i s_j t².
- term (j, j+l) if valid: s_j t * h_{j+l} + cross if j+l=i.
- term (x, x+l) where x = i-l and i-l != i: h_x * (h_i+s_i t) if i-l !=j? delta = s_i t * h_x; if x=j cross.
 etc. More simply, since each variable appears in at most two product terms; for pair l where both i and j are endpoints of a product with lag l, i.e. j = i+l or i = j+l. Then one product term involves both and delta has cross coefficient s_i*s_j = -1 times? P term new product - old. If product term is subtracted, ΔA includes -ΔP. For both endpoints: ΔP = s_i h_j t + s_j h_i t + s_i s_j t². s_i s_j = -1. ΔA = -ΔP = -s_i h_j t - s_j h_i t + t². But earlier A_l convex +t². Good.
No other cross terms.

Building function:
```
def build_pair_coef(h, c, i, j):
    n=len(h); L=2*n-1
    b0 = np.zeros(n) # not needed
    b1 = np.zeros(L)
    b2 = np.zeros(L)
    add_single_coef(b1,b2,h,i,+1.0,n)
    add_single_coef(b1,b2,h,j,-1.0,n)
    # add cross for paired nonzero lags
    l = j - i
    if l != 0:
       m = n-1 + l
       if 0 <= m < L: b2[m] += 1.0
       m2 = n-1 - l
       if 0<=m2<L: b2[m2] += 1.0
    return b1,b2
```
Wait cross should be added exactly once for each paired orientation? There are two lags l and -l, each has one product term if both endpoints. For l=j-i, m=n-1+l. For l=i-j, m2. Add +1 to each. Good.
But if i,j adjacent? l=±1; both lags valid. Good.

`add_single_coef(b1,b2,h,p,s,n)`:
```
def add_single_coef(b1,b2,h,p,s,n):
    hp=h[p]
    b2[n-1] += -1.0
    b1[n-1] += s*(1.0-2.0*hp)
    if p>0:
       b1[n-1-p:n-1] += s*(1.0-h[:p])
    m=n-p-1
    if m>0:
       b1[n:2*n-p-1] += s*(1.0-h[p+1:n])
    if p>0:
       b1[n:n+p] += -s*h[:p]
    if m>0:
       b1[p:n-1][::-1] += -s*h[p+1:n]
```
This is same as apply_single but no hp update, no c0. For p, l=0 c2 -1; for pair sum -2. Good.

```
   if ignore0: v[n-1] = -1e100
   return float(v.max())
```

`minimize_pair_F` procedure:
```
def pair_minimize(c,b1,b2,lo,hi):
    if hi-lo < 1e-14: return 0, cmax
    v0=eval(0)
    # grid points from lo to hi inclusive N=16
    xs = np.linspace(lo, hi, 17)
    vals=[eval(x) for x in xs]
    # include 0 if not close to grid? add maybe
    if distance to grid >?? simply add 0 if not close.
    best_i = argmin(vals)
    if vals[best_i] >= v0 - tol_eval: return 0, v0
    best_t=xs[best_i]; best_v=vals[best_i]
    # local golden for best_i and maybe endpoints if low
    candidates_indices = {best_i}
    if vals[0] < v0 - tol: candidates_indices.add(0)
    if vals[-1] < v0 - tol: candidates_indices.add(-1)
    if len(candidates)<2: add neighbor? maybe top second.
    for idx in candidates (max 2):
       if idx==0: a=xs[0]; b=xs[1]
       elif idx==len(xs)-1: a=xs[-2]; b=xs[-1]
       else: a=xs[idx-1]; b=xs[idx+1]
       # if t=0 inside bracket and is okay? include.
       t,v = golden_F(c,b1,b2,a,b, max_eval=15, initial_best=(best_t,best_v))
       update
    return best_t,best_v
```
Golden_F:
```
def golden_F(c,b1,b2,a,b,max_eval=15, init_t=None, init_v=None):
   best_t=init_t; best_v=init_v if not None else eval(init)
   # check endpoints
   for x in (a,b,0 if within):
      v=...
   gr...
   x1=a+gr*(b-a)? Standard.
   evals=...
   while b-a>1e-12 and evals<max_eval:
      if f1 <= f2: b=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=a+gr*(b-a); f1=eval(x1)
      else: a=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=a+gr*(b-a); f2=eval(x2)
   check mid
   return best
```
Need no lambdas.

But if F nonunimodal in bracket, golden may ignore other min. Bracket small; okay.

```
def finalize_h(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,float)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    s=0.5*n
    diff=s-h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-8:
       h=project_box_sum(h,n)
    else:
       # adjust minimally: if diff>0 add to first non-saturated, else subtract
       if abs(diff)>1e-13:
          idxs = np.arange(n)
          # choose index with most room/opposite
          if diff>0:
             idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
             add=min(diff,1.0-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add
          else:
             idx=int(np.argmax(h))
             sub=min(-diff,h[idx]); h[idx]-=sub
          # If still diff, small loop maybe
    return h
```

Let's refine `pair_minimize`:
```
def pair_minimize(c,b1,b2,lo,hi,tol):
    # evaluate 0 and grid vectorized
    G=25  # includes endpoints
    xs = np.linspace(lo,hi,G)
    arr = c[None,:] + b1[None,:]*xs[:,None] + b2[None,:]*(xs[:,None]**2)
    vals = arr.max(axis=1)
    # If t=0 not exactly on grid, add
    # find index nearest to 0 if within
    t0 = 0.0
    v0 = float(np.max(c))  # since b at 0 zero, c max
    # Include 0: if not too close to xs, append? Simpler: best among vals and v0.
    best_t=0.0; best_v=v0
    gi=int(np.argmin(vals))
    if vals[gi] < best_v:
       best_t=float(xs[gi]); best_v=float(vals[gi])
    if best_v >= v0 - tol:
       return best_t,best_v
    # candidate indices: best, and any endpoint with val < v0 - tol
    cand=[gi]
    if vals[0] < v0 - tol: cand.append(0)
    if vals[-1] < v0 - tol: cand.append(G-1)
    # also second best maybe
    # sort cand by vals
    cand sorted? small loop.
    for idx in cand[:2]:
      if idx == 0: a=xs[0]; b=xs[1]
      elif idx == G-1: a=xs[-2]; b=xs[-1]
      else: a=xs[idx-1]; b=xs[idx+1]
      t,v=golden_pair(c,b1,b2,a,b, max_eval=14, init_t=best_t, init_v=best_v)
      if v<best_v: best_v=v; best_t=t
    return best_t,best_v
```
If best grid point is not local and function decreases to endpoint, endpoint candidate covers. If best at boundary, golden bracket adjacent. Good.

Golden pair can also use vectorized? scalar.

```
def golden_pair(c,b1,b2,a,b,maxeval=14,init_t=None,init_v=None):
   if b-a <1e-15: return init
   def? no closures.
   best_t=init_t; best_v=init_v if not None else eval(init)
   # check endpoints and midpoint maybe
   for x in (a,b,(a+b)/2):
      if x<lo? But bracket within.
      v=eval...
   # golden
   gr=0.618...
   x1=a+gr*(b-a); x2=b-gr*(b-a) ? Standard two interior.
   f1=eval(x1); f2=eval(x2)
   for ...:
      if f1 <= f2:
         b = x2; x2 = x1; f2=f1; x1 = a + gr*(b-a); f1=eval(x1)
      else:
         a = x1; x1 = x2; f1=f2; x2 = b - gr*(b-a); f2=eval(x2)
   final mid.
```

Now, pair_polish code with coefficients:
```
def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=np.array(h,float)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    c=direct_corr(h)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
    L=2*n-1
    b1=np.zeros(L); b2=np.zeros(L)
    tol = 1e-9
    while time.time() < deadline:
       cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax < best_c:
          best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
       w,_=smooth_weights(c, alpha)
       g=gradient_from_weights(h,w)
       # Generate pairs list
       low=np.argsort(g)[:K]
       high=np.argsort(-g)[:K]
       pairs=[]
       for ii in low:
          for jj in high:
             if ii!=jj:
               sc=g[jj]-g[ii]
               if sc > 1e-9:
                 pairs.append((sc,int(ii),int(jj)))
               else:
                 # also negative direction? If sc negative, the reverse pair (jj,ii) will be in another combination maybe
                 pass
       pairs.sort(reverse=True)
       # Limit
       max_pairs=1200
       if len(pairs)>max_pairs: pairs=pairs[:max_pairs]
       # Add random pairs if time maybe use rng
       nrand = 200 if n<=500 else 100
       for _ in range(nrand):
          ii=int(rng.randint(n)); jj=int(rng.randint(n))
          if ii!=jj:
             # score absolute gradient diff; if positive
             sc=abs(g[ii]-g[jj])
             if sc > 1e-8:
               pairs.append((sc,ii,jj)) # sign not important due full interval
       # process
       improved=False
       for sc,ii,jj in pairs:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          # Optional skip if objective already improved? no
          lo = max(-h[ii], h[jj]-1.0)
          hi = min(1.0-h[ii], h[jj])
          if hi-lo < 1e-13: continue
          build_pair_coef(h,ii,jj,n,b1,b2)
          t,bv = pair_minimize(c,b1,b2,lo,hi,tol)
          if bv < cmax - tol:
             # clip t
             if t < lo: t=lo
             if t > hi: t=hi
             h[ii] += t; h[jj] -= t
             # fix bounds tiny
             if h[ii] < 0.0: h[ii]=0.0
             elif h[ii] > 1.0: h[ii]=1.0
             if h[jj] < 0.0: h[jj]=0.0
             elif h[jj] > 1.0: h[jj]=1.0
             c = direct_corr(h)
             cmax = float(c.max())
             improved=True
             if cmax < best_c:
                best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
       if not improved:
          break
    return best_h, best_c
```

`pairs.sort(reverse=True)` sorts by score then ii, jj. Fine.

Now, `lbfgs_refine`: It uses `_lbfgs_objective`, which calls `direct_corr`. In objective, if `x` has sum not exact, direct_corr still. Smooth weights. Need stable logsumexp. Implement:
```
def smooth_max_value(c, alpha):
   m=float(c.max())
   if alpha <=0: return m
   s=alpha*(c-m)
   smax=s.max()
   # smax 0
   return m + (smax + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s-smax))))) / alpha
```
But if alpha large, s up to 0, exp(s) ≤1, sum ≤L. log okay. If alpha small, s negative large, exp underflows to 0 maybe, log? If all underflow except max, sum≈1. good. If m is nan, return inf.

Gradient: weights from `smooth_weights`. It uses `s=(c-m)/alpha`, subtract max; same.

`_lbfgs_objective(x,n,alpha,mu)`:
```
x = np.asarray(x)
if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100
c=direct_corr(x)
val=smooth_max_value(c,alpha)
s=n/2.0
diff=float(np.sum(x)-s)
val += mu*diff*diff
return float(val)
```
`_lbfgs_gradient` similar:
```
w,m=smooth_weights(c,alpha); g=gradient_from_weights(x,w); diff=sum-s; g += 2*mu*diff*ones; return g
```

```
def try_alternate_n(best_h,best_n,best_C,deadline,rng):
   # return best_h,n,C
   if best_n < 200:
      candidates=[int(round(best_n*1.5)), int(round(best_n*2))]
   elif best_n < 600:
      candidates=[int(round(best_n*1.5)), int(round(best_n*2))]
   else:
      candidates=[int(round(best_n*1.25))]
   for ntry in candidates:
      if ntry > 1000 or ntry < 50: continue
      if time.time() > deadline - 180: break
      # interpolate
      x_old = (np.arange(best_n)+0.5)*2.0/best_n
      x_new = (np.arange(ntry)+0.5)*2.0/ntry
      h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, best_h)
      h = project_box_sum(h,ntry)
      # quick LBFGS
      h,_ = lbfgs_refine(h,ntry,min(time.time()+120, deadline-100),rng)
      c = direct_c5(h)
      if c < best_C:
         best_h=h; best_n=ntry; best_C=c
      # quick pair if time
      if time.time() < deadline-80:
         h2,c2u = pair_polish(h,ntry,min(time.time()+120, deadline-40),rng)
         c2=c2u*2.0/ntry
         if c2 < best_C: update
   return best_h,best_n,best_C
```

```
def direct_corr(h):
    n = len(h)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
```

```
def direct_c5(h):

    return float(np.max(direct_corr(h)) * (2.0 / n))
```

5. `project_box_sum(h,n)` bisection. If n=0? return.
```
def project_box_sum(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if n <=0: return h
    s=0.5*n
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): h=np.full(n,0.5)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # if sum close, still maybe adjust? Use bisection to exact.
    lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0
    tau = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    for _ in range(80):
       x = np.clip(h - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
       sm = float(x.sum())
       if sm < s:
          hi = tau
       else:
          lo = tau
       tau = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    x = np.clip(h - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
    # residual
    diff = s - float(x.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
       if diff > 0:
          room = 1.0 - x
          tot = float(room.sum())
          if tot > 1e-15:
             x += (diff / tot) * room
       else:
          tot = float(x.sum())
          if tot > 1e-15:
             x += (diff / tot) * x
       x = np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    return x
```
Potential: Bisection tau range if h already feasible sum. It may find a tau not 0 if plateau due saturated all? If h feasible with some values strictly between, unique tau. If all 0/1 and sum=s (n even), any tau in [0,1]? Actually clip(h-tau) for h in {0,1}: sum = #ones (for tau between0 and1) =s. Bisection condition sm < s? false, so it moves lo up; final tau near hi? Could output all ones zero? It may pick tau near max? Let's test: lo=-1,hi=2. tau=.5 sum=s, sm >=s => lo=.5. tau=1.25 x=0? if ones clip 0? Actually 1-1.25<0 =>0 sum0 <s => hi=1.25. It converges around tau where sum just >=s, maybe tau≈1? final x all 0 if tau slightly <1 gives ones 1? If tau near1, ones at 1-tau small, sum below s? Need exact. The bisection may not return original binary h. That can badly change pair-polished h if we project after each or final. For pairPolish we don't project. For initial/final if h has saturated binary, projection could alter. Avoid projecting a feasible h unless sum out of bounds. Use `project_box_sum` only when needed (initial maybe feasible). But for L-BFGS res, if h near binary, project may change? L-BFGS h likely not exactly binary? Could. Need a safer projection that minimizes L2 from v with sum constraint. The tau of water-filling for binary v: if sum target equals #ones, any tau in [0,1] gives same sum but different L2? Actually for v=1, clip(1-tau) = 1-tau for tau∈[0,1]; for v=0, clip(-tau)=0. Sum = count_ones*(1-tau). To get sum=s=count_ones, tau=0. The function sum(tau) is decreasing from count to0 for tau∈[0,1]. Unique tau=0. I earlier thought plateau; no, because 1-tau changes. If tau>1, zeros. So unique. Good. If h includes values >1? after clip. If all h in {0,1}, sum(s) unique tau=0. Bisection okay: at tau=.5 sum=s/2 <s, hi=.5; converge to0. Condition: sm< s -> hi=tau. Starting lo=min-1=-1, hi=max+1=2. At tau=.5 sm=s/2< => hi=.5. tau=-.25 sm>s? ones 1.25 ->1, sum=s (since clip <=1) actually all ones ->sum=s, sm>= => lo=-.25. Then tau=.125 sm=s*(0.875)< hi=.125. 6. `finalize_feasible(h,n)`: use minimal adjust instead of full projection if already close. Let's implement:
```
def finalize_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   s=0.5*n
   diff=s - float(h.sum())
   if abs(diff) > 1e-7:
       return project_box_sum(h,n)
   if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       # find best index to adjust
       if diff > 0:
          cum = 1.0 - h
          idx = int(np.argmax(cum))
          add = min(float(diff), float(cum[idx]))
          h[idx] += add
       else:
          idx = int(np.argmax(h))
          sub = min(float(-diff), float(h[idx]))
          h[idx] -= sub
       # if still residual due to saturation, distribute to few
       diff2 = s - float(h.sum())
       if abs(diff2) > 1e-13:
          # small loop over indices
          for k in range(n):
             if diff2 > 0 and h[k] < 1.0:
                add = min(diff2, 1.0-h[k]); h[k]+=add; diff2-=add; if abs(diff2)<1e-15: break
             elif diff2 < 0 and h[k] > 0.0:
                sub = min(-diff2, h[k]); h[k]-=sub; diff2+=sub; if abs(diff2)<1e-15: break
   return h
```
Use at final to avoid projection altering. But if h already used in pairPolish, sum close. Good.

7. smooth functions.
```
def smooth_weights(c, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    L = len(c)
    w = np.zeros(L)
    if alpha <= 1e-12 or L==0:
       w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0; return w,m
    s = (c - m) / alpha
    smax = float(np.max(s))
    w = np.exp(s - smax)
    ss = float(w.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
       w.fill(0.0); w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
       w /= ss
    return w,m
```
`math.isfinite` okay.

`smooth_max_value(c, alpha)`:
```
def smooth_max_value(c, alpha):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <=1e-12: return m
    s=alpha*(c-m)
    smax=float(np.max(s))
    val = m + (smax + math.log(float(np.sum(np.exp(s-smax))))) / alpha
    return val
```
If alpha very small, s-smax negative, exp okay. If sum 0? not.

`gradient_from_weights` as above.

8. Coeff building.
```
def add_single_coef(b1,b2,h,p,s,n):
    hp=h[p]
    b2[n-1] += -1.0
    b1[n-1] += s*(1.0 - 2.0*hp)
    if p > 0:
        b1[n-1-p:n-1] += s*(1.0 - h[:p])
    m = n - p - 1
    if m > 0:
        b1[n:2*n-p-1] += s*(1.0 - h[p+1:n])
    if p > 0:
        b1[n:n+p] += -s*h[:p]
    if m > 0:
        b1[p:n-1][::-1] += -s*h[p+1:n]
```
Need `b2[n-1]` exists if n=1. yes.

```
def build_pair_coef(b1,b2,h,i,j,n):
    b1.fill(0.0); b2.fill(0.0)
    add_single_coef(b1,b2,h,i,1.0,n)
    add_single_coef(b1,b2,h,j,-1.0,n)
    l = j - i
    if l != 0:
       m1 = n - 1 + l
       if 0 <= m1 < 2*n - 1: b2[m1] += 1.0
       m2 = n - 1 - l
       if 0 <= m2 < 2*n - 1: b2[m2] += 1.0
```

9. `eval_pair_t(c,b1,b2,t)` and `golden_pair` and `pair_minimize`.
```
def eval_pair_t(c,b1,b2,t):
    return float(np.max(c + b1*t + b2*t*t))
```
`golden_pair`:
```
def golden_pair(c,b1,b2,a,b,max_eval=14,init_t=None,init_v=None):
    if b - a <= 1e-15:
       if init_t is None: return None? better return a,eval
    best_t = init_t if init_t is not None else 0.5*(a+b)
    if init_v is not None: best_v=init_v
    else: best_v=eval_pair_t(c,b1,b2,best_t)
    # check endpoints and midpoint
    for x in (a,b,0.5*(a+b)):
       v=eval_pair_t(c,b1,b2,float(x))
       if v < best_v:
          best_v=v; best_t=float(x)
    if max_eval <= 4:
       return best_t,best_v
    gr=(math.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    x1 = a + gr*(b-a)
    x2 = b - gr*(b-a)
    f1 = eval_pair_t(c,b1,b2,float(x1))
    f2 = eval_pair_t(c,b1,b2,float(x2))
    if f1 < best_v: best_v=f1; best_t=x1
    if f2 < best_v: best_v=f2; best_t=x2
    evals=5
    while b - a > 1e-12 and evals < max_eval:
       if f1 <= f2:
          b = x2
          x2 = x1
          f2 = f1
          x1 = a + gr*(b-a)
          f1 = eval_pair_t(c,b1,b2,float(x1))
       else:
          a = x1
          x1 = x2
          f1 = f2
          x2 = b - gr*(b-a)
          f2 = eval_pair_t(c,b1,b2,float(x2))
       evals += 1
       if f1 < best_v: best_v=f1; best_t=x1
       if f2 < best_v: best_v=f2; best_t=x2
    return best_t,best_v
```
Need handle `init_t` maybe None. In pair_minimize init exists. Good.

`pair_minimize`:
```
def pair_minimize(c,b1,b2,lo,hi,tol=1e-10):
    if hi - lo < 1e-14:
       return 0.0, float(np.max(c))
    v0 = float(np.max(c))
    best_t=0.0; best_v=v0
    G = 25
    xs = np.linspace(lo, hi, G)
    # Evaluate grid vectorized
    # For memory, if L large, still okay.
    ts2 = xs[:,None]*xs[:,None]
    arr = c[None,:] + b1[None,:]*xs[:,None] + b2[None,:]*ts2
    vals = arr.max(axis=1)
    gi = int(np.argmin(vals))
    if vals[gi] < best_v:
       best_t = float(xs[gi]); best_v = float(vals[gi])
    # Include 0 if not in grid within tol
    # If 0 strictly inside and not close to grid, evaluate exact (v0 already)
    if best_v >= v0 - tol:
       return best_t, best_v
    # candidates
    cand = [gi]
    if vals[0] < v0 - tol and 0 not in cand:
       cand.append(0)
    if vals[-1] < v0 - tol and len(cand)<3:
       cand.append(len(xs)-1)
    # maybe add second best if not local? Use simple: find index of second minimum among first 5? Could skip.
    # Sort cand by vals ascending
    # Since max 3, manual sort
    if len(cand) > 1:
       # insertion sort
       for a in range(1,len(cand)):
          key=cand[a]; keyv=float(vals[key]); bb=a-1
          while bb>=0 and float(vals[cand[bb]]) > keyv:
             cand[bb+1]=cand[bb]; bb-=1
          cand[bb+1]=key
    # refine top 2
    count=0
    for idx in cand:
       if count >= 2: break
       count += 1
       G_len = len(xs)
       if idx <= 0:
          aa = xs[0]; bb = xs[1] if G_len > 1 else xs[0]
       elif idx >= G_len-1:
          aa = xs[-2]; bb = xs[-1]
       else:
          aa = xs[idx-1]; bb = xs[idx+1]
       t,v = golden_pair(c,b1,b2,float(aa),float(bb),max_eval=14,init_t=best_t,init_v=best_v)
       if v < best_v:
          best_v=float(v); best_t=float(t)
    return best_t,best_v
```
If `best_v` is from v0 but grid not lower than tol, returns 0. Good.
Potential: if 0 inside bracket and v0 best but grid best slightly lower >tol? We only refine if grid best < v0 - tol. If v0 true best, skip. Good.

If grid best lower but actual best at 0? impossible if grid best lower than v0. But maybe grid approximation error? v0 exact; if grid says lower, refine.

```
def fix_sum_after_pair(h,n,i,j):
    s=0.5*n
    diff=s - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) <= 1e-13: return h
    # try other indices
    for k in range(n):
       if k==i or k==j: continue
       if diff>0 and h[k] < 1.0:
           add=min(diff,1.0-h[k]); h[k]+=add; diff-=add
       elif diff<0 and h[k] >0.0:
           sub=min(-diff,h[k]); h[k]-=sub; diff+=sub
       if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    # if cannot, use i/j carefully preserving maybe
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       h=project_box_sum(h,n)
    return h
```
But project_box_sum could change i/j. Only if clip bigger. Not likely. Include top-level.

Now, `lbfgs_refine`: Need maybe call `finalize_feasible` after res, not project_box_sum to avoid altering? Use `finalize_feasible`. But L-BFGS x may be infeasible sum; finalize. If sum diff >1e-7, project. Good.

```
def lbfgs_refine(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h = finalize_feasible(h,n)
    best = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax_unscaled(h)
    if n < 2: return best,best_c
    bounds=[]
    for _ in range(n): bounds.append((0.0,1.0))
    # choose configs
    configs = [(5.0,1.0),(20.0,10.0),(100.0,10.0),(500.0,100.0)]
    for idx in range(len(configs)):
        if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
        alpha, mu = configs[idx]
        maxiter = 60 if n <= 400 else 30
        try:
            res = minimize(_lbfgs_objective, h, jac=_lbfgs_gradient, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-16, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxfun':maxiter*5, 'maxls':20})
            h2 = finalize_feasible(res.x,n)
            c2 = cmax_unscaled(h2)
            if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c2; best = h2; h = h2
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best, best_c
```
The `args` for gradient include n,alpha,mu; gradient function signature `def _lbfgs_gradient(x,n,alpha,mu):` okay.

```
def slsqp_refine(h,n,deadline,rng):
   h=finalize_feasible(h,n)
   best... 
   if n>400: return
   bounds...
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_slsqp_con,'jac':_slsqp_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   for alpha in [10.0,50.0,200.0]:
      if time>deadline-5: break
      try:
        res=minimize(_slsqp_objective,h,jac=_slsqp_gradient,args=(n,alpha),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':20,'ftol':1e-12})
        h2=finalize_feasible(res.x,n); c2=...
      except: pass
```

11. `try_alternate_n`.

```
def try_alternate_n(best_h,best_n,best_C,deadline,rng):
    cands=[]
    if best_n <= 0: return...
    if best_n < 250:
       cands.append(int(round(best_n*1.5)))
       cands.append(int(round(best_n*2.0)))
    elif best_n < 600:
       cands.append(int(round(best_n*1.5)))
       cands.append(int(round(best_n*2.0)))
    elif best_n < 1200:
       cands.append(int(round(best_n*1.25)))
    # cap 1000
    # unique list without lambdas/check
    seen=[]
    for cn in cands:
       if cn < 50 or cn > 1000: continue
       if cn not in seen: seen.append(cn)
    for ntry in seen:
       if time.time() > deadline - 120: break
       x_old=(np.arange(best_n)+0.5)*(2.0/best_n)
       x_new=(np.arange(ntry)+0.5)*(2.0/ntry)
       h=np.interp(x_new,x_old,best_h)
       h=project_box_sum(h,ntry)
       # quick lbfgs
       t_lb = min(time.time()+100.0, deadline-80.0)
       if t_lb > time.time()+10:
          h,_=lbfgs_refine(h,ntry,t_lb,rng)
          c=direct_c5(h)
          if c < best_C:
             best_h=h; best_n=ntry; best_C=c
       if time.time() > deadline - 80: break
       t_pair = min(time.time()+120.0, deadline-30.0)
       if t_pair > time.time()+10:
          h2,cu=pair_polish(h,ntry,t_pair,rng)
          c2=cu*(2.0/ntry)
```

`fix_sum_after_pair`: top-level. Implementation:
```
def fix_sum_after_pair(h,n,i,j):
    s=0.5*n
    diff=s-float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff)<=1e-13: return h
    for k in range(n):
       if k==i or k==j: continue
       if diff>0 and h[k] < 1.0:
          add = min(float(diff), float(1.0-h[k]))
          h[k]+=add; diff-=add
       elif diff<0 and h[k] > 0.0:
          sub = min(float(-diff), float(h[k]))
          h[k]-=sub; diff+=sub
       if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       h=project_box_sum(h,n)
    return h
```
If clipping of i itself due t beyond, sum diff maybe -t overshoot; other index adjust. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def direct_corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def direct_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(np.max(direct_corr(h)) * (2.0 / n))


def cmax_unscaled(h):
    return float(np.max(direct_corr(h)))


def project_box_sum(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if n <= 0:
        return h
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        h = np.full(n, 0.5)

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = 0.5 * float(n)

    lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0
    tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)

    for _ in range(80):
        x = np.clip(h - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
        sm = float(x.sum())
        if sm < s:
            hi = tau
        else:
            lo = tau
        tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)

    x = np.clip(h - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = s - float(x.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - x
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot > 1e-15:
                x += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = float(x.sum())
            if tot > 1e-15:
                x += (diff / tot) * x
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x


def finalize_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = 0.5 * float(n)
    diff = s - float(h.sum())

    if abs(diff) > 1e-7:
        return project_box_sum(h, n)

    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            cum = 1.0 - h
            idx = int(np.argmax(cum))
            add = min(float(diff), float(cum[idx]))
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(float(-diff), float(h[idx]))
            h[idx] -= sub

        diff2 = s - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff2) > 1e-13:
            for k in range(n):
                if diff2 > 0.0 and h[k] < 1.0:
                    add = min(float(diff2), float(1.0 - h[k]))
                    h[k] += add
                    diff2 -= add
                    if abs(diff2) < 1e-15:
                        break
                elif diff2 < 0.0 and h[k] > 0.0:
                    sub = min(float(-diff2), float(h[k]))
                    h[k] -= sub
                    diff2 += sub
                    if abs(diff2) < 1e-15:
                        break
    return h


def fix_sum_after_pair(h, n, i, j):
    s = 0.5 * float(n)
    diff = s - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
        return h

    for k in range(n):
        if k == i or k == j:
            continue
        if diff > 0.0 and h[k] < 1.0:
            add = min(float(diff), float(1.0 - h[k]))
            h[k] += add
            diff -= add
        elif diff < 0.0 and h[k] > 0.0:
            sub = min(float(-diff), float(h[k]))
            h[k] -= sub
            diff += sub
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break

    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    return h


def smooth_weights(c, alpha):
    L = len(c)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    w = np.zeros(L)
    if alpha <= 1e-12 or L == 0:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w, m

    s = (c - m) / float(alpha)
    smax = float(np.max(s))
    w = np.exp(s - smax)
    ss = float(w.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
        w.fill(0.0)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= ss
    return w, m


def smooth_max_value(c, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <= 1e-12:
        return m
    s = float(alpha) * (c - m)
    smax = float(np.max(s))
    val = m + (smax + math.log(max(float(np.sum(np.exp(s - smax))), 1e-300))) / float(alpha)
    return float(val)


def gradient_from_weights(h, w):
    n = len(h)
    g = np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(len(w)):
        wm = float(w[m])
        if wm < 1e-15:
            continue
        l = m - (n - 1)
        if l >= 0:
            nv = n - l
            if nv > 0:
                g[:nv] += wm * (1.0 - h[l:])
                if l == 0:
                    g[:] += -wm * h
                else:
                    g[l:] += -wm * h[:nv]
        else:
            kk = -l
            nv = n - kk
            if nv > 0:
                g[kk:] += wm * (1.0 - h[:nv])
                g[:nv] += -wm * h[kk:]
    return g


def add_single_coef(b1, b2, h, p, s, n):
    hp = float(h[p])
    b2[n - 1] += -1.0
    b1[n - 1] += s * (1.0 - 2.0 * hp)

    if p > 0:
        b1[n - 1 - p:n - 1] += s * (1.0 - h[:p])

    mat = n - p - 1
    if mat > 0:
        b1[n:2 * n - p - 1] += s * (1.0 - h[p + 1:n])

    if p > 0:
        b1[n:n + p] += -s * h[:p]

    if mat > 0:
        b1[p:n - 1][::-1] += -s * h[p + 1:n]


def build_pair_coef(b1, b2, h, i, j, n):
    b1.fill(0.0)
    b2.fill(0.0)
    add_single_coef(b1, b2, h, i, 1.0, n)
    add_single_coef(b1, b2, h, j, -1.0, n)

    l = int(j) - int(i)
    if l != 0:
        L = 2 * n - 1
        m1 = n - 1 + l
        if 0 <= m1 < L:
            b2[m1] += 1.0
        m2 = n - 1 - l
        if 0 <= m2 < L:
            b2[m2] += 1.0


def eval_pair_t(c, b1, b2, t):
    t = float(t)
    return float(np.max(c + b1 * t + b2 * t * t))


def golden_pair(c, b1, b2, a, b, max_eval=14, init_t=None, init_v=None):
    a = float(a)
    b = float(b)
    if b - a <= 1e-15:
        if init_t is None:
            t = a
        else:
            t = float(init_t)
        v = float(init_v) if init_v is not None else eval_pair_t(c, b1, b2, t)
        return t, v

    if init_t is None:
        best_t = 0.5 * (a + b)
    else:
        best_t = float(init_t)
    best_v = float(init_v) if init_v is not None else eval_pair_t(c, b1, b2, best_t)

    for x in (a, b, 0.5 * (a + b)):
        v = eval_pair_t(c, b1, b2, float(x))
        if v < best_v:
            best_v = v
            best_t = float(x)

    if max_eval <= 4:
        return best_t, best_v

    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    x1 = a + gr * (b - a)
    x2 = b - gr * (b - a)

    f1 = eval_pair_t(c, b1, b2, float(x1))
    f2 = eval_pair_t(c, b1, b2, float(x2))
    if f1 < best_v:
        best_v = f1
        best_t = float(x1)
    if f2 < best_v:
        best_v = f2
        best_t = float(x2)

    evals = 5
    while b - a > 1e-12 and evals < max_eval:
        if f1 <= f2:
            b = x2
            x2 = x1
            f2 = f1
            x1 = a + gr * (b - a)
            f1 = eval_pair_t(c, b1, b2, float(x1))
        else:
            a = x1
            x1 = x2
            f1 = f2
            x2 = b - gr * (b - a)
            f2 = eval_pair_t(c, b1, b2, float(x2))
        evals += 1
        if f1 < best_v:
            best_v = f1
            best_t = float(x1)
        if f2 < best_v:
            best_v = f2
            best_t = float(x2)

    return best_t, best_v


def pair_minimize(c, b1, b2, lo, hi, tol=1e-9):
    lo = float(lo)
    hi = float(hi)
    if hi - lo < 1e-14:
        return 0.0, float(np.max(c))

    v0 = float(np.max(c))
    best_t = 0.0
    best_v = v0

    G = 25
    base = np.linspace(lo, hi, G)
    xs = base

    if lo <= 0.0 <= hi:
        exact = False
        pos = int(np.searchsorted(base, 0.0))
        if pos < len(base) and abs(float(base[pos]) - 0.0) < 1e-14:
            exact = True
        if pos > 0 and abs(float(base[pos - 1]) - 0.0) < 1e-14:
            exact = True
        if not exact:
            ins = pos
            xs = np.concatenate((base[:ins], np.array([0.0]), base[ins:]))

    ts = np.asarray(xs, dtype=float)
    arr = c[None, :] + b1[None, :] * ts[:, None] + b2[None, :] * (ts[:, None] ** 2)
    vals = arr.max(axis=1)

    gi = int(np.argmin(vals))
    if float(vals[gi]) < best_v:
        best_v = float(vals[gi])
        best_t = float(ts[gi])

    if best_v >= v0 - tol:
        return best_t, best_v

    cand = [gi]
    if float(vals[0]) < v0 - tol and 0 not in cand:
        cand.append(0)
    if float(vals[-1]) < v0 - tol and (len(xs) - 1) not in cand:
        cand.append(len(xs) - 1)

    for pos in range(1, len(cand)):
        key = cand[pos]
        keyv = float(vals[key])
        back = pos - 1
        while back >= 0 and float(vals[cand[back]]) > keyv:
            cand[back + 1] = cand[back]
            back -= 1
        cand[back + 1] = key

    count = 0
    glen = len(xs)
    for idx in cand:
        if count >= 2:
            break
        count += 1
        if idx <= 0:
            aa = float(xs[0])
            bb = float(xs[1]) if glen > 1 else aa
        elif idx >= glen - 1:
            aa = float(xs[-2]) if glen > 1 else float(xs[-1])
            bb = float(xs[-1])
        else:
            aa = float(xs[idx - 1])
            bb = float(xs[idx + 1])

        t, v = golden_pair(c, b1, b2, aa, bb, max_eval=14, init_t=best_t, init_v=best_v)
        if float(v) < best_v:
            best_v = float(v)
            best_t = float(t)

    return best_t, best_v


def pair_polish(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if n <= 0:
        return h, cmax_unscaled(h)

    c = direct_corr(h)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax

    L = 2 * n - 1
    b1 = np.zeros(L)
    b2 = np.zeros(L)

    alpha = float(2.0 + 0.005 * n)
    K = int(max(15, min(70, 15 + n // 20)))
    max_grad_pairs = int(max(150, min(1200, 200000 // max(1, n))))
    tol = 1e-9

    while time.time() < deadline:
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if cmax < best_c:
            best_c = cmax
            best_h = h.copy()

        w, _ = smooth_weights(c, alpha)
        g = gradient_from_weights(h, w)

        low = np.argsort(g)[:K]
        high = np.argsort(-g)[:K]

        pairs = []
        for ii in low:
            ii = int(ii)
            for jj in high:
                jj = int(jj)
                if ii == jj:
                    continue
                sc = float(g[jj]) - float(g[ii])
                if sc > 1e-9:
                    pairs.append((sc, ii, jj))
                elif sc < -1e-9:
                    pairs.append((-sc, jj, ii))

        pairs.sort(reverse=True)
        if len(pairs) > max_grad_pairs:
            pairs = pairs[:max_grad_pairs]

        improved = False

        for sc, ii, jj in pairs:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break

            lo = max(-float(h[ii]), float(h[jj]) - 1.0)
            hi = min(1.0 - float(h[ii]), float(h[jj]))
            if hi - lo < 1e-13:
                continue

            build_pair_coef(b1, b2, h, ii, jj, n)
            t, bv = pair_minimize(c, b1, b2, lo, hi, tol)

            if bv < cmax - tol:
                if t < lo:
                    t = lo
                elif t > hi:
                    t = hi

                h[ii] += t
                h[jj] -= t

                if h[ii] < 0.0:
                    h[ii] = 0.0
                elif h[ii] > 1.0:
                    h[ii] = 1.0
                if h[jj] < 0.0:
                    h[jj] = 0.0
                elif h[jj] > 1.0:
                    h[jj] = 1.0

                h = fix_sum_after_pair(h, n, ii, jj)
                c = direct_corr(h)
                cmax = float(c.max())
                improved = True
                if cmax < best_c:
                    best_c = cmax
                    best_h = h.copy()

        if not improved and time.time() < deadline:
            nrand = int(max(20, min(220, 70000 // max(1, n))))
            rand_pairs = []
            for _ in range(nrand):
                ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                if ii != jj:
                    sc = abs(float(g[ii]) - float(g[jj]))
                    rand_pairs.append((sc, ii, jj))

            rand_pairs.sort(reverse=True)

            for sc, ii, jj in rand_pairs:
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break

                lo = max(-float(h[ii]), float(h[jj]) - 1.0)
                hi = min(1.0 - float(h[ii]), float(h[jj]))
                if hi - lo < 1e-13:
                    continue

                build_pair_coef(b1, b2, h, ii, jj, n)
                t, bv = pair_minimize(c, b1, b2, lo, hi, tol)

                if bv < cmax - tol:
                    if t < lo:
                        t = lo
                    elif t > hi:
                        t = hi

                    h[ii] += t
                    h[jj] -= t

                    if h[ii] < 0.0:
                        h[ii] = 0.0
                    elif h[ii] > 1.0:
                        h[ii] = 1.0
                    if h[jj] < 0.0:
                        h[jj] = 0.0
                    elif h[jj] > 1.0:
                        h[jj] = 1.0

                    h = fix_sum_after_pair(h, n, ii, jj)
                    c = direct_corr(h)
                    cmax = float(c.max())
                    improved = True
                    if cmax < best_c:
                        best_c = cmax
                        best_h = h.copy()
                if improved:
                    break

        if not improved:
            break

    return best_h, best_c


def _lbfgs_objective(x, n, alpha, mu):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100
    c = direct_corr(x)
    val = smooth_max_value(c, alpha)
    diff = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
    val += float(mu) * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def _lbfgs_gradient(x, n, alpha, mu):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.zeros(n)
    c = direct_corr(x)
    w, _ = smooth_weights(c, alpha)
    g = gradient_from_weights(x, w)
    diff = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
    g += 2.0 * float(mu) * diff
    return g


def _slsqp_objective(x, n, alpha):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100
    c = direct_corr(x)
    return smooth_max_value(c, alpha)


def _slsqp_gradient(x, n, alpha):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.zeros(n)
    c = direct_corr(x)
    w, _ = smooth_weights(c, alpha)
    return gradient_from_weights(x, w)


def _slsqp_con(x, n):
    return np.array([float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))])


def _slsqp_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones((1, n))


def make_bounds(n):
    b = []
    for _ in range(int(n)):
        b.append((0.0, 1.0))
    return b


def lbfgs_refine(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = finalize_feasible(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax_unscaled(h)
    if n < 2:
        return best, best_c

    bounds = make_bounds(n)
    configs = [
        (5.0, 1.0),
        (20.0, 10.0),
        (100.0, 10.0),
        (500.0, 100.0)
    ]

    for alpha, mu in configs:
        if time.time() > deadline - 5:
            break
        maxiter = 70 if n <= 400 else 35
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lbfgs_objective,
                h,
                jac=_lbfgs_gradient,
                args=(n, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-11,
                    "maxfun": maxiter * 5,
                    "maxls": 20
                }
            )
            h2 = finalize_feasible(res.x, n)
            c2 = cmax_unscaled(h2)
            if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c2
                best = h2
                h = h2
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best, best_c


def slsqp_refine(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = finalize_feasible(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax_unscaled(h)
    if n < 2 or n > 450:
        return best, best_c

    bounds = make_bounds(n)
    cons = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": _slsqp_con,
            "jac": _slsqp_jac,
            "args": (n,)
        }
    ]

    for alpha in (10.0, 50.0, 200.0):
        if time.time() > deadline - 5:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _slsqp_objective,
                h,
                jac=_slsqp_gradient,
                args=(n, alpha),
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 20,
                    "ftol": 1e-12
                }
            )
            h2 = finalize_feasible(res.x, n)
            c2 = cmax_unscaled(h2)
            if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c2
                best = h2
                h = h2
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best, best_c


def make_cosine_h(n):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(math.pi * (x - 1.0))
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_box_sum(h, n)


def try_alternate_n(best_h, best_n, best_C, deadline, rng):
    if best_n <= 0:
        return best_h, best_n, best_C

    cand = []
    if best_n < 250:
        cand.append(int(round(best_n * 1.5)))
        cand.append(int(round(best_n * 2.0)))
    elif best_n < 650:
        cand.append(int(round(best_n * 1.5)))
        cand.append(int(round(best_n * 2.0)))
    elif best_n < 1300:
        cand.append(int(round(best_n * 1.25)))

    seen = []
    for cn in cand:
        if cn < 50 or cn > 1000:
            continue
        if cn not in seen:
            seen.append(cn)

    for ntry in seen:
        if time.time() > deadline - 120:
            break

        x_old = (np.arange(best_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(best_n))
        x_new = (np.arange(ntry) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(ntry))
        h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, best_h)
        h = project_box_sum(h, ntry)

        t_lb = min(time.time() + 120.0, deadline - 90.0)
        if t_lb > time.time() + 15:
            h2, _ = lbfgs_refine(h, ntry, t_lb, rng)
            c2 = direct_c5(h2)
            if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_C:
                best_h = h2
                best_n = ntry
                best_C = c2

        t_pair = min(time.time() + 150.0, deadline - 40.0)
        if t_pair > time.time() + 20:
            h3, cu = pair_polish(h, ntry, t_pair, rng)
            c3 = float(cu) * (2.0 / float(ntry))
            if math.isfinite(c3) and c3 < best_C:
                best_h = h3
                best_n = ntry
                best_C = c3

    return best_h, best_n, best_C


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(20.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    deadline = t0 + total
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_n = 0
    best_C = float("inf")

    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        if h0.size > 0:
            n0 = int(h0.size)
            h0 = finalize_feasible(h0, n0)
            best_h = h0.copy()
            best_n = n0
            best_C = direct_c5(h0)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_h is None:
        n0 = 300
        best_h = make_cosine_h(n0)
        best_n = n0
        best_C = direct_c5(best_h)

    if not math.isfinite(best_C):
        n0 = 300
        best_h = make_cosine_h(n0)
        best_n = n0
        best_C = direct_c5(best_h)

    d1, d2, d3, d4 = 400.0, 200.0, 300.0, 180.0
    dsum = d1 + d2 + d3 + d4
    if 0.95 * total < dsum:
        scale = max(0.05, (0.95 * total) / dsum)
        d1 *= scale
        d2 *= scale
        d3 *= scale
        d4 *= scale

    end1 = min(t0 + d1, deadline - 5.0)
    if end1 > t0 + 15:
        h1, cu1 = pair_polish(best_h, best_n, end1, rng)
        c1 = float(cu1) * (2.0 / float(len(h1)))
        if math.isfinite(c1) and c1 < best_C:
            best_C = c1
            best_h = h1

    end2 = min(time.time() + d2, deadline - 5.0)
    if end2 > time.time() + 15:
        h2, _ = lbfgs_refine(best_h, best_n, end2, rng)
        c2 = direct_c5(h2)
        if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_C:
            best_C = c2
            best_h = h2

        if time.time() < end2 - 10 and best_n <= 450:
            h3, _ = slsqp_refine(best_h, best_n, end2, rng)
            c3 = direct_c5(h3)
            if math.isfinite(c3) and c3 < best_C:
                best_C = c3
                best_h = h3

    end3 = min(time.time() + d3, deadline - 5.0)
    if end3 > time.time() + 15:
        h4, cu4 = pair_polish(best_h, best_n, end3, rng)
        c4 = float(cu4) * (2.0 / float(len(h4)))
        if math.isfinite(c4) and c4 < best_C:
            best_C = c4
            best_h = h4

    end4 = min(time.time() + d4, deadline - 5.0)
    if end4 > time.time() + 60:
        best_h, best_n, best_C = try_alternate_n(best_h, best_n, best_C, end4, rng)

    end5 = deadline - 3.0
    if time.time() < end5 - 5:
        h5, cu5 = pair_polish(best_h, best_n, end5, rng)
        c5 = float(cu5) * (2.0 / float(len(h5)))
        if math.isfinite(c5) and c5 < best_C:
            best_C = c5
            best_h = h5

    best_h = finalize_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_C = direct_c5(best_h)

    if not math.isfinite(best_C):
        best_h = make_cosine_h(300)
        best_n = 300
        best_C = direct_c5(best_h)

    return best_h, float(best_C), int(best_n)
```
